\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[2E\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\left(\frac{f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}+\frac{f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-2E\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\left(\frac{f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}+\frac{f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-2E(f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}|\mathbf{z}_{i})-2f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}+4\tau\right].

Note that
J/\sqrt{n}
is an average of i.i.d. samples of random vector
(\mathbf{z}_{i}^{\top},f_{i},W_{i})^{\top}
, and that
\tau=E(f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i})=E(f_{i}(1,\pi)-f_{i}(0,\pi))
. Therefore, to show
J=o_{p}(1)
, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle J_{1}=var

\displaystyle\left[2E\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\left(\frac{f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}+\frac{f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-2E\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\left(\frac{f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}+\frac{f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-2E(f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}|\mathbf{z}_{i})-2f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}+4\tau\right]=o(1).

(81)

By letting
S_{1}(u)=E(f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}|\mathbf{z}_{i}=u)
, and using the law of total expectation and integration by substitution, we derive that

\displaystyle 2E\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\left(\frac{f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}+\frac{f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=2\int\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\left(\frac{S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{j})I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}+\frac{f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)p(\mathbf{z}_{j})d\mathbf{z}_{j}

\displaystyle=2\int K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)\left(\frac{S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}+\frac{f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})>\tilde{b})}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})}\right)p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})d\mathbf{u}

\displaystyle=2\int K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)\frac{p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}d\mathbf{u}+2\int K(\mathbf{u})f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+hu)>\tilde{b})d\mathbf{u}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle J_{1}

\displaystyle=var\left(2\int K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)\frac{p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}d\mathbf{u}-2S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\int K(\mathbf{u})f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+hu)>\tilde{b})d\mathbf{u}-2f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2var\left(2\int K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)\frac{p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}d\mathbf{u}-2S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i})\right)

\displaystyle+2var\left(2\int K(\mathbf{u})f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})>\tilde{b})d\mathbf{u}-2f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim 8\iint\left|K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)\frac{p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})I_{i}S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})-p(\mathbf{z}_{i})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right|^{2}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})dud\mathbf{z}_{i}

\displaystyle+8\iint\left|K\left(u\right)(1-I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})>\tilde{b}))\right|^{2}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{i},

where in the last inequality we apply Jensen’s inequality to exchange the order of integration and squaring, and we also use the boundedness of
E(f_{i}^{2}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}|\mathbf{z}_{i})
. Since
p(\mathbf{z}),\nabla p(\mathbf{z}),K(\mathbf{u}),S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i})
, the support of
K(\mathbf{u})
are bounded and
\tilde{h}=o(\tilde{b})
, we have

\displaystyle\left|K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)\frac{p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})I_{i}S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})-p(\mathbf{z}_{i})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right|^{2}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle K^{2}(\mathbf{u})p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\left(1+I_{i}\frac{p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right)^{2}\lesssim K^{2}(\mathbf{u})p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\left(1+\frac{\tilde{h}^{2}(\mathbf{1}^{\top}\mathbf{u})^{2}}{\tilde{b}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle K^{2}(\mathbf{u})p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\left(1+{(\mathbf{1}^{\top}\mathbf{u})^{2}}\right),

(82)

which is integrable. Moreover,

\displaystyle\left|K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)(1-I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})>\tilde{b}))\right|^{2}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\lesssim K^{2}(\mathbf{u})p(\mathbf{z}_{i})

which is also integrable. As a result, we can apply the dominated convergence theorem to obtain that
J_{1}=o(1)
, and consequently,
J=o_{p}(1)
.
Therefore, (
Proof.
) is equlaivent to

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(U_{11}-EU_{11})

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}+E(f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)R_{i}|\mathbf{z}_{i})-2\tau\right)+o_{p}(1).

(83)

By (
78
), (
83
), and the Slutsky’s theorem, the final result follows.
∎

Lemma 9
.

Under the assumptions for Theorem
6
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\delta_{12}-E\delta_{12})=\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi}\right)\right)+o_{p}(1),

where

\displaystyle\delta_{12}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{1}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi}\right)I\left(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b}\right).

Proof.

Let
R_{j}=\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi},I_{j}=I\left(p(\mathbf{z}_{j})>\tilde{b}\right)
for
1\leq j\leq n
,

\displaystyle U_{12}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n(n-1)\tilde{h}^{p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j\neq i}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\frac{I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}.

Then by the symmetry of
K(\cdot)
,

\displaystyle\delta_{12}-\frac{n-1}{n}U_{12}-E(\delta_{12}-\frac{n-1}{n}U_{12})

\displaystyle=\frac{2K(0)}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}\left(\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}-E\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right),

and its variance

\displaystyle var(\delta_{12}-\frac{n-1}{n}U_{12})

\displaystyle=\frac{4K^{2}(\mathbf{0})}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}E\left[\left(\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}-E\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\frac{f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)R_{j}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}-E\frac{f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)R_{j}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{4K^{2}(\mathbf{0})}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\left[\left(\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}-E\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4K^{2}(\mathbf{0})}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left[\left(\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}-E\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\frac{f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)R_{j}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}-E\frac{f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)R_{j}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4K^{2}(\mathbf{0})}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\left(\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4K^{2}(\mathbf{0})}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left[\left|\left(\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right)\left(\frac{f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)R_{j}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{4K^{2}(\mathbf{0})}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left|\frac{f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)R_{i}I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\right|E\left|\frac{f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)R_{j}I_{j}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\left(f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left[\left|E(|f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)||W_{i})\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)E(|f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)||W_{j})\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\right|\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left|E(|f_{i}^{(1)}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)||W_{i})\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)\right|E\left|E(|f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)||W_{j})\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}+\frac{1}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{i<j}\left(E\left|\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\right|+E\left|\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right|E\left|\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}+\frac{1}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{i<j}\left(\sqrt{E\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)^{2}}\sqrt{E\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}+\frac{1}{n^{4}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}\frac{n^{2}}{n\rho_{n}}\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}\rho_{n}},

where we use (i)
\max_{w=0,1}E[|f_{i}^{(1)}(w,\pi)|^{2}|w]<\infty
, (ii)
I_{i}/p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\leq 1/\tilde{b}
for
1\leq i\leq n
, (iii) the boundedness of
R_{i},I_{i},M_{i}/N_{i}
for
1\leq i\leq n
, (iv) Lemma 15(c) in
Li and Wager, 2022
. By Assumption
5
and the Markov inequality, we have
\delta_{12}-\frac{n-1}{n}U_{12}-E(\delta_{12}-\frac{n-1}{n}U_{12})=o_{p}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})
. As a result,

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\delta_{12}-E\delta_{12})-\sqrt{n}(U_{12}-EU_{12})=o_{p}(1).

(84)

We now use the projection technique to approximate
U_{12}
. Let

r_{n}=\frac{2}{n(n-1)\tilde{h}^{p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j\neq i}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\frac{I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}E\left(K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right).

(85)

We prove that
U_{12}-r_{n}
is negligible by showing that
E\left(U_{12}-r_{n}\right)^{2}=o(n^{-1})
.

\displaystyle E(U_{12}-r_{n})^{2}

\displaystyle=E\left[\frac{2}{n(n-1)}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j:j\neq i}\frac{I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right.\right.

\displaystyle-\left.\left.E\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right)\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{4}{n^{2}(n-1)^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j:j\neq i}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{m:m\neq l}E\frac{\sum_{k:k\neq j}E_{kj}(W_{k}-\pi)\sum_{q:q\neq m}E_{qm}(W_{q}-\pi)}{N_{j}N_{m}}

\displaystyle\left(\frac{I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}-E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{I_{l}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{l})\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{l}-\mathbf{z}_{m}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{m}^{(1)}\left(W_{m},\pi\right)R_{m}-E\left(\frac{I_{l}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{l})\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{l}-\mathbf{z}_{m}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{m}^{(1)}\left(W_{m},\pi\right)R_{m}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{l}\right)\right).

(86)

We note that most of the terms in (
86
) are zero because
EW_{k}=\pi
for all
1\leq k\leq n
,
\{W_{i}\}
are i.i.d.,
\{\mathbf{z}_{i}\}
are i.i.d., and
\{W_{i}\}
are independent of
\{\mathbf{z}_{i}\}
. Specifically, the summation term is nonzero only if both of the following conditions occur:

1.

m=j
, or
j=l,m=i
;

2.

k=m,q=j
, or
k=q
.

Moreover, by Assumption
5
we have

\displaystyle\max_{w=0,1}E\left(\left|\frac{I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right|\bigg|W_{j}=w\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\frac{I_{i}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})\tilde{h}^{p}}|K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)|p(\mathbf{z}_{i})p(\mathbf{z}_{j})d\mathbf{z}_{i}d\mathbf{z}_{j}\leq\int|K(\mathbf{u})|p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{i},

(87)

which is bounded. Then (
86
) reduces to

\displaystyle E(U_{12}-r_{n})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{4}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j:j\neq i}\sum_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{m:m\neq l}E\frac{\sum_{k:k\neq j}E_{kj}\sum_{q:q\neq m}E_{qm}}{N_{j}N_{m}}

\displaystyle\quad I(\{m=j\}\cup\{j=l,m=i\})I(\{k=q\}\cup\{k=m,q=j\})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{4}}E\sum_{i,j,l:i\neq j,l\neq j}\frac{\sum_{k:k\neq j}E_{kj}^{2}}{N_{j}^{2}}+\frac{1}{n^{4}}E\sum_{i,j:i\neq j}\frac{\sum_{k:k\neq i,j}E_{kj}E_{ki}+E_{ij}}{N_{j}N_{i}}.

(88)

By Lemma 15 and the proof of Lemma 17 in
Li and Wager, 2022
,

P(N_{i}<cn\rho_{n})<\exp(-Cn\rho_{n}),E(1/N_{i})\leq C/(n\rho_{n}),E\sum_{i,j:i\neq j}\sum_{k:k\neq i,j}E_{kj}E_{ki}=O(n^{3}\rho_{n}^{2})

for some constants
c,C>0
. Therefore,
E(U_{12}-r_{n})^{2}=O(n^{-2}\rho_{n}^{-1}+n^{-3})=o(n^{-1})
, which indicates that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(U_{12}-EU_{12})-\sqrt{n}(r_{n}-Er_{n})=o_{p}(1).

(89)

We now refine (
85
) to simplify it. Note that

\displaystyle r_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\frac{1}{n-1}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)+\tilde{r}_{n1}+\tilde{r}_{n2}+\tilde{r}_{n3},

where

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{n1}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{1}{n(n-1)}\sum_{i:i\neq j}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right)\right.

\displaystyle-\left.\frac{1}{n}E\left(\frac{I_{j}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}K\left(\mathbf{0}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{j}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{n2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right)\right.

\displaystyle-\left.E\left(\frac{I_{j}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)\right),

\displaystyle\tilde{r}_{n3}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\left(E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)-E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)\right).

We now prove that
\tilde{r}_{n1}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}),\tilde{r}_{n2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}),\tilde{r}_{n3}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
, so that
r_{n}
can be well-approximated by
\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)
.

For
\tilde{r}_{n1}
:
By (
Proof.
) and
|\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi|<1
, we have
E|\tilde{r}_{n1}|\lesssim O(n^{-1})
. Therefore,
\tilde{r}_{n1}=O_{p}(n^{-1})
.

For
\tilde{r}_{n2}
:
By Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle E|\tilde{r}_{n2}|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{E\left(\frac{2}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\right)^{2}}\sqrt{var\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right)\right)}.

For the first term in the right-hand-side, we have

\displaystyle E\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}E\left(\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\right)

\displaystyle=O(\frac{1}{n^{2}\rho_{n}})+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left(\frac{\sum_{k\neq i}E_{ik}(W_{k}-\pi)\sum_{l\neq j}E_{lj}(W_{l}-\pi)}{N_{i}N_{j}}\right)

\displaystyle=O(\frac{1}{n^{2}\rho_{n}})+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left(\frac{\sum_{k\neq i,j}E_{ik}E_{kj}}{N_{i}N_{j}}\pi(1-\pi)\right)

\displaystyle=O(\frac{1}{n^{2}\rho_{n}}+\frac{1}{n})=O(\frac{1}{n}),

(90)

where we apply Lemmas 15 and the proof of Lemma 17 in
Li and Wager, 2022
, as similarly done in the derivations in (
Proof.
).

For the second term in the right-hand-side, we have

\displaystyle var\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}var\left(E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}E\left(\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right)^{2}E((f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right))^{2}|\mathbf{z}_{j})\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\int{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)d\mathbf{z}_{i}d\mathbf{z}_{j}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\int{p(\mathbf{z}_{j})}K^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}\right)d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{j}\lesssim\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}=o(1).

Therefore,
E|\tilde{r}_{n2}|=o(n^{-1/2})
.

For
\tilde{r}_{n3}
:
similar to the previous derivations, it suffices to notice that

\displaystyle E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)-E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=E\left(\int\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{p}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)d\mathbf{z}_{i}f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}-f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)

\displaystyle=E\left(\left(\int I(p(\mathbf{z}_{j}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})>\tilde{b})K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)d\mathbf{u}-1\right)E(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}|\mathbf{z}_{j})\right)\to 0,

where we use the boundedness of
E(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}|\mathbf{z}_{j}),K(\mathbf{u})
, the fact that
\tilde{h},\tilde{b}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
, and the dominated convergence theorem. Then we have
\tilde{r}_{n3}=o_{p}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(r_{n}-Er_{n})=\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right).

(91)

By combining (
91
) with (
84
), (
89
), the final result follows.
∎

Lemma 10
.

Under the assumptions for Theorem
6
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\delta_{21}-E\delta_{21})=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(E(f_{i}(1,\pi)-f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}\right)\right.

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\left.E(f_{i}(1,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}-E(f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}-3\tau\right)+o_{p}(1),

where

\displaystyle\delta_{21}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{j}W_{k}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})(1-W_{k})}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right).

Proof.

The proof is divided into three parts. In
Step 1
, we associate
\delta_{21}
with a U-statistic
U_{21}
. In
Step 2
, we approximate
U_{21}
using conditional expectation. In
Step 3
, we further approximate the expression to derive the final result.

Step 1.
Let
R_{j}=\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi},I_{j}=I(p(\mathbf{z}_{j})>\tilde{b}),R_{jk}=\frac{W_{j}W_{k}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})(1-W_{k})}{(1-\pi)^{2}}
for
1\leq j,k\leq n
,

\displaystyle U_{21}=\frac{6}{n(n-1)(n-2)}\sum_{i<j<k}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{6}\left(K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{i}R_{jk}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}(f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)+f_{k}\left(W_{k},\pi\right))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{j}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{j}-\mathbf{z}_{i}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{j}R_{ik}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{j})}(f_{k}\left(W_{k},\pi\right)+f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{k}-\mathbf{z}_{i}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{k}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{k}R_{ij}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{k})}(f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)+f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right))\right).

Then by the symmetry of
K(\cdot)
,

\displaystyle\delta_{21}-\frac{(n-1)(n-2)}{n^{2}}U_{21}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i,j:i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}(W_{j},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{j}^{2}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})^{2}}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i,j:i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K(\mathbf{0})K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)(f_{j}(W_{j},\pi)+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi))R_{ij}

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}(\mathbf{0})f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{i}^{2}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{i})^{2}}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right).

To show that
\sqrt{n}(\delta_{21}-E\delta_{21})-\sqrt{n}(U_{21}-EU_{21})=o_{p}(1)
, it suffices to show that
E|\delta_{21}-\frac{(n-1)(n-2)}{n^{2}}U_{21}|=o(1/\sqrt{n})
.

First, consider the term
\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}(W_{j},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{j}^{2}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})^{2}}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right)
, we derive that

\displaystyle E\left|\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}(W_{j},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{j}^{2}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})^{2}}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}\pi^{2}(1-\pi)^{2}}E\left|\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)E(f_{j}(W_{j},\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{j})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}}\sum_{i\neq j}E\left|\frac{1}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\iint p(\mathbf{z}_{j})K^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}\right)d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{j}\lesssim\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}=o(n^{-1/2}),

(92)

where the last equality is due to Assumption
5
.

Second, consider the term
\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K(\mathbf{0})K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)(f_{j}(W_{j},\pi)+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi))R_{ij}
. Similar to the derivations in (
Proof.
), we have

\displaystyle E\left|\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K(\mathbf{0})K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)(f_{j}(W_{j},\pi)+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi))R_{ij}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}E\left|\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\iint p(\mathbf{z}_{j})|K(\mathbf{u})|d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{j}=o(1/\sqrt{n}).

Finally, for the term
\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}(\mathbf{0})f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{i}^{2}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{i})^{2}}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}E\left|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}(\mathbf{0})f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{i}^{2}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{i})^{2}}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}=o(1/\sqrt{n}).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\delta_{21}-E\delta_{21})-\sqrt{n}(U_{21}-EU_{21})=o_{p}(1).

(93)

Step 2.
Similar to Step 2 in Lemma
8
, by the proof of Theorem 4.2.1 in
Korolyuk, 2013
and the
c_{r}
-inequality for expectation, to show

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(U_{21}-EU_{21})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(g_{21}(\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i})-Eg_{21}(\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i})\right)+o_{p}(1),

(94)

where

\displaystyle g_{21}(\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}E\left(K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{i}R_{jk}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}(f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)+f_{k}\left(W_{k},\pi\right))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{j}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{j}-\mathbf{z}_{i}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{j}R_{ik}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{j})}(f_{k}\left(W_{k},\pi\right)+f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{k}-\mathbf{z}_{i}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{k}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{k}R_{ij}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{k})}(f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)+f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right))\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i}\right),

it suffices to prove that

\displaystyle E\left(K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{i}R_{jk}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\right)^{2}=o(n).

(95)

To see (
95
), notice that by the boundedness of
R_{jk},E(|f_{j}(W_{j},\pi)||\mathbf{z}_{j})
, we have

\displaystyle E\left(K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{i}R_{jk}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{h^{4p}}\int K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{i}}{p^{3}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}p(\mathbf{z}_{j})p(\mathbf{z}_{k})d\mathbf{z}_{i}d\mathbf{z}_{j}d\mathbf{z}_{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{h^{2p}b^{3}}\int K^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{1}\right)K^{2}\left(\mathbf{u}_{2}\right)p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}d\mathbf{z}_{i}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{h^{2p}b^{3}}=o(n).

Step 3.
We now refine (
94
) to show that the summands on the right-hand side can be approximated by
E(f_{i}(1,\pi)-f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}\right)+E(f_{i}(1,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}-E(f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}-3\tau
, which do not vary with
n
. Let

S_{2}(u)=E(f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}|\mathbf{z}_{j})\bigg|_{\mathbf{z}_{j}=u},S_{3}(v,u)=E(f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}|W_{j},\mathbf{z}_{j})\bigg|_{W_{j}=v,\mathbf{z}_{j}=u}.

Consider the difference term

\displaystyle J=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[g_{21}(\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i})-Eg_{21}(\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i})-E(f_{i}(1,\pi)-f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}\right)-E(f_{i}(1,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}+E(f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}+3\tau\right].

Note that
J/\sqrt{n}
is an average of functions of i.i.d. samples of random vector
(\mathbf{z}_{i}^{\top},f_{i},W_{i})^{\top}
. By the definition of
\tau
in Theorem
1
, each summand of
J
has a mean of zero. Therefore, to show
J=o_{p}(1)
, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle J_{1}=var

\displaystyle\left[g_{21}(\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i})-Eg_{21}(\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i})-E(f_{i}(1,\pi)-f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\right.

\displaystyle\left.-f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}+\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}\right)-E(f_{i}(1,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}+E(f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}+3\tau\right]=o(1).

(96)

By the law of total expectation and integration by substitution, we derive that

\displaystyle g_{21}(\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}E\left(K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{i}R_{jk}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}(f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)+f_{k}\left(W_{k},\pi\right))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{j}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{j}-\mathbf{z}_{i}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{j}R_{ik}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{j})}(f_{k}\left(W_{k},\pi\right)+f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right))\right.

\displaystyle\left.+K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{k}-\mathbf{z}_{i}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{k}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\frac{I_{k}R_{ij}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{k})}(f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)+f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right))\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i},W_{i},f_{i}\right),

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int K\left(\mathbf{u}_{1}\right)K\left(\mathbf{u}_{2}\right)\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}[S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})+S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})]p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int K\left(\mathbf{u}_{1}\right)K\left(\mathbf{u}_{2}\right)\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})}

\displaystyle[S_{3}(W_{i},\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}]p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int K\left(\mathbf{u}_{1}\right)K\left(\mathbf{u}_{2}\right)\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})}

\displaystyle[f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}+S_{3}(W_{i},\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})]p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}.

Recall that
S_{2}(u)=E(f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}|\mathbf{z}_{j})\bigg|_{\mathbf{z}_{j}=u},S_{3}(v,u)=E(f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}|W_{j},\mathbf{z}_{j})\bigg|_{W_{j}=v,\mathbf{z}_{j}=u}
. We have

\displaystyle S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}+S_{3}(W_{i},\mathbf{z}_{i}))

\displaystyle=E(f_{i}(1,\pi)-f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}\right)+E(f_{i}(1,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}-E(f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}.

Then

\displaystyle J_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim E\left(\int K\left(\mathbf{u}_{1}\right)K\left(\mathbf{u}_{2}\right)\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}[S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})+S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})]p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}-2S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+E\left(\int K\left(\mathbf{u}_{1}\right)K\left(\mathbf{u}_{2}\right)\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})}[S_{3}(W_{i},\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}]\right.

\displaystyle\left.p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}-[S_{3}(W_{i},\mathbf{z}_{i})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\left(K\left(\mathbf{u}_{1}\right)K\left(\mathbf{u}_{2}\right)\left[\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}[S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})+S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})]p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})-2S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle p(\mathbf{z}_{i})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}d\mathbf{z}_{i}

\displaystyle+\int\left(K\left(\mathbf{u}_{1}\right)K\left(\mathbf{u}_{2}\right)\left[\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.[S_{3}(W_{i},\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}]p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})-[S_{3}(W_{i},\mathbf{z}_{i})+f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}]\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle p(\mathbf{z}_{i})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}d\mathbf{z}_{i},

where in the last inequality we apply Jensen’s inequality to exchange the order of integration and squaring. Similar to the arguments in (
Proof.
), using the fact that
\tilde{h}=o(\tilde{b})
, we can find integrable dominating functions for the two terms on the right-hand side of the above equation, allowing us to apply the dominated convergence theorem. By the dominated convergence theorem, we have
J_{1}=o(1)
. Then the proof is complete by referring (
Proof.
), (
94
) and (
93
).

∎

Lemma 11
.

Under the assumptions for Theorem
6
, we have

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\delta_{22}-E\delta_{22})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi}\right)\right)+o_{p}(1),

where

\displaystyle\delta_{22}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{W_{j}W_{k}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})(1-W_{k})}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right).

Proof.

Let
R_{j}=\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi},I_{j}=I(p(\mathbf{z}_{j})>\tilde{b}),R_{jk}=\frac{W_{j}W_{k}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})(1-W_{k})}{(1-\pi)^{2}}
for
1\leq j,k\leq n
,

\displaystyle U_{22}=\frac{1}{n(n-1)(n-2)}\sum_{i,j,k\text{ are distinct }}\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)R_{jk}.

Then

\displaystyle\delta_{22}-\frac{(n-1)(n-2)}{n^{2}}U_{22}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}(W_{j},\pi)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{j}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\sum_{i\neq j}\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K(\mathbf{0})K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\left[f_{j}^{(1)}(W_{j},\pi)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)+f_{i}^{(1)}(W_{i},\pi)\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)\right]R_{ij}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K^{2}(\mathbf{0})\left(\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{i}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{1-W_{i}}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right).

Similar to the proofs in Lemma
9
, we have that

\displaystyle E\left|\delta_{22}-\frac{(n-1)(n-2)}{n^{2}}U_{22}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\sum_{i,j:i\neq j}E\frac{K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}E\left|\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right|+\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\sum_{i,j:i\neq j}E\frac{|K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)|}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}E\left|\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}E\left|\frac{M_{i}}{N_{i}}-\pi\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}\sqrt{n\rho_{n}}}\int K^{2}(\mathbf{u})p(\mathbf{z}_{j})d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{j}+\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}\sqrt{n\rho_{n}}}\int|K(\mathbf{u})|p(\mathbf{z}_{j})d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{j}+\frac{1}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}\sqrt{n\rho_{n}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}\sqrt{n\rho_{n}}}+\frac{1}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}\sqrt{n\rho_{n}}}=o(n^{-1/2}).

Therefore,

\sqrt{n}(\delta_{22}-E\delta_{22})-\sqrt{n}(U_{22}-EU_{22})=o_{p}(1).

We now use the projection technique to approximate
U_{22}
. Let

r_{n}=\frac{1}{n(n-1)(n-2)\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i,j,k\text{ are distinct }}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}E\left(K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right).

(97)

We prove that
U_{22}-r_{n}
is negligible by showing that
E\left(U_{22}-r_{n}\right)^{2}=o(n^{-1})
.

\displaystyle E\left(U_{22}-r_{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=E\left(\frac{1}{n(n-1)(n-2)}\sum_{i,j,k\text{ are distinct }}\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}-E\left[K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right]\right\}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{6}}\sum_{i,j,k\text{ are distinct }}\sum_{i_{1},j_{1},k_{1}\text{ are distinct }}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\frac{I_{i_{1}}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}})}\frac{\sum_{l:l\neq j}(W_{l}-\pi)E_{jl}\sum_{l_{1}:l_{1}\neq j_{1}}(W_{l_{1}}-\pi)E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}-E\left[K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i}\right]\right\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\{K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{j_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{k_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j_{1}}^{(1)}\left(W_{j_{1}},\pi\right)R_{j_{1}k_{1}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-E\left[K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{j_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{k_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j_{1}}^{(1)}\left(W_{j_{1}},\pi\right)R_{j_{1}k_{1}}\bigg|\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}\right]\right\}\right).

(98)

Similar to the arguments after (
86
), most of the summands are zero. We notice that for the summand to be nonzero, the eight indices
i,j,k,i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l,l_{1}
can have at most six distinct values. Then (
Proof.
) reduces to

\displaystyle E\left(U_{22}-r_{n}\right)^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{6}}(A+B+C+D)

where

\displaystyle A=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{M}}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\frac{I_{i_{1}}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}})}\frac{(W_{l}-\pi)E_{jl}(W_{l_{1}}-\pi)E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{j_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{k_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j_{1}}^{(1)}\left(W_{j_{1}},\pi\right)R_{j_{1}k_{1}}\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}

\displaystyle=\{i,j,k,i_{1},j_{1},k_{1},l,l_{1}\text{ have at most six distinct values },i,j,k,\text{ distinct},i_{1},j_{1},k_{1}\text{ distinct}\},

and
B,C,D
are defined in the obvious way. For
A
, since

\sup_{\mathbf{z}_{j}}\max_{w=0,1}E(|f_{j}^{(1)}(W_{j},\pi)|^{2}|W_{j}=w,\mathbf{z}_{j}),R_{jk},W_{l}-\pi\text{ are bounded },

we note that

\displaystyle A\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{M}}E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\frac{I_{i_{1}}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}})}\frac{E_{jl}E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{j_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{k_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{\mathcal{M}}E\left(\frac{E_{jl}E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right)E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}\frac{I_{i_{1}}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{j_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i_{1}}-\mathbf{z}_{k_{1}}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right).

Case 1:
i,j,k,i_{1},j_{1},k_{1}
are distinct. Since

\displaystyle E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right)\leq\int K(\mathbf{u}_{1})K(\mathbf{u}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{i}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}d\mathbf{z}_{i}

(99)

is bounded, we have

\displaystyle A\lesssim\frac{1}{\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{\mathcal{M}}E\left(\frac{E_{jl}E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right).

Case 2: At least one of
i_{1},j_{1},k_{1}
is equal to one of
i,j,k
. Since
n\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}\to\infty
by Assumption
5
, and
K(\cdot)
is bounded, we have

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{n^{2}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{3}}\sum_{i,j,k,\text{ distinct}}E\left(\frac{E_{jl}E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right)E\left(\frac{I_{i}}{\tilde{h}^{2p}p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{\mathcal{M}}E\left(\frac{E_{jl}E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right),

where in the last inequality we use (
99
). The analysis for
B
,
C
, and
D
is similar. Finally, we conclude that

\displaystyle E\left(U_{22}-r_{n}\right)^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{6}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{\mathcal{M}}E\left(\frac{E_{jl}E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right).

By Lemma 15 in
Li and Wager, 2022
,

E(E_{ij})\lesssim\rho_{n},P(N_{i}<cn\rho_{n})<\exp(-Cn\rho_{n})

for some constants
c,C>0
.
We then derive that when
n
is sufficiently large,

\displaystyle E\left(U_{22}-r_{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{6}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{\mathcal{M}}E\left(\frac{E_{jl}E_{j_{1}l_{1}}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{6}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{\mathcal{M}}E\left(\frac{E_{jl}}{N_{j}N_{j_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{6}\tilde{b}^{2}}\sum_{\mathcal{M}}[\frac{\rho_{n}}{n^{2}\rho_{n}^{2}}+\rho_{n}\exp(-Cn\rho_{n})]

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}\rho_{n}\tilde{b}^{2}}=o(n^{-1}),

(100)

where in the last equality we use the fact that
n\rho_{n}\tilde{b}^{2}\to\infty
which is implied by Assumption
2
and Assumption
5
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(U_{22}-EU_{22})-\sqrt{n}(r_{n}-Er_{n})=o_{p}(1).

By following the procedure from (
89
) to (
91
), we have that

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(r_{n}-Er_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{jk}\right)+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)E\left(f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right)+o_{p}(1).

Then the proof is complete.
∎

Lemma 12
.

Under the assumptions for Theorem
6
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbf{z}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\left|\hat{p}(\mathbf{z})-p(\mathbf{z})\right|

\displaystyle=O_{a.s.}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n)}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}}+\tilde{h}^{q}\right),

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbf{z}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\left|\hat{p}_{1}(\mathbf{z})-p(\mathbf{z})\right|

\displaystyle=O_{a.s.}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n)}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}}+\tilde{h}^{q}\right),

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbf{z}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\left|\hat{p}_{2}(\mathbf{z})-p(\mathbf{z})\right|

\displaystyle=O_{a.s.}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n)}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}}+\tilde{h}^{q}\right),

where

\displaystyle\hat{p}(\mathbf{z})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right),

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{1}(\mathbf{z})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\pi}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)W_{j},

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{2}(\mathbf{z})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}(1-\pi)}\sum_{j=1}^{n}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)(1-W_{j}).

Proof.

We only show that

\sup_{\mathbf{z}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\left|\hat{p}_{1}(\mathbf{z})-p(\mathbf{z})\right|=O_{a.s.}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n)}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}}+\tilde{h}^{q}\right),

as the other two results can be derived similarly. First, the conditions for Theorem 5 in
Hansen, 2008
are satisfied under our Assumption
5
. Consequently, we have

\sup_{\mathbf{z}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}}\left|\hat{p}_{1}(\mathbf{z})-E\hat{p}_{1}(\mathbf{z})\right|=O_{a.s.}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n)}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}}\right).

Second, by integration by parts and a change of variables, we derive that

\displaystyle E\hat{p}_{1}(\mathbf{z})-p(\mathbf{z})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\pi}\sum_{j=1}^{n}E\left[K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)W_{j}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}E\left[K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{p}}\int K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)p(\mathbf{z}_{j})d\mathbf{z}_{j}

\displaystyle=\int K(\mathbf{u})p(\mathbf{z}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})d\mathbf{u}=p(\mathbf{z})+O(\tilde{h}^{q}),

where the final equality is by a
q
-th order Taylor series expansion and using the assumed properties of the kernel and
p(\mathbf{z})
.
Then the results follows.
∎

Lemma 13
.

Let

\delta_{12}=\frac{2}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{1}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi}\right)I\left(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b}\right),

\displaystyle\delta_{22}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)

\displaystyle\left(\frac{W_{j}W_{k}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})(1-W_{k})}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right).

Under the assumptions for Theorem
6
, we have
E\delta_{12}=0,E\delta_{12}=o(n^{-1/2})
.

Proof.

For
\delta_{12}
, note that we let
G_{n}
denote the random graph, and
M_{j}-\pi N_{j}=\sum_{k:k\neq j}(W_{k}-\pi)E_{kj}
given
G_{n}
. Since
W_{k}
’s are sampled independently, we have
E\delta_{12}=0
.
For
\delta_{22}
, by noting the above argument for
\delta_{12}
, we derive that

\displaystyle E\delta_{22}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left\{\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{M_{j}}{N_{j}}-\pi\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\frac{W_{j}W_{k}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})(1-W_{k})}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left\{\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)E_{kj}\frac{W_{k}-\pi}{N_{j}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\frac{W_{j}W_{k}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})(1-W_{k})}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right)\right\}.

Noting that
E(E_{kj}/N_{j})=O(1/n)
which can be derived using similar arguments as those in equation (
Proof.
), and that
E_{kj}/N_{j}
is determined by graph
G_{n}
, we then have

\displaystyle|E\delta_{22}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left|\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}^{(1)}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left|\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n}E\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{2p}}\frac{1}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{3}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{2}}E\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{2p}}\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)\right|+\frac{1}{n^{2}}E\left|\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{2p}}\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(0\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{2p}}\frac{1}{\tilde{b}^{2}}K^{2}\left(0\right)

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{n\tilde{b}}+\frac{1}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}+\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}\right)=O(1/\sqrt{n}).

∎

Lemma 14
.

Let

\displaystyle\delta_{11}=\frac{2}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\frac{1}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi}\right)I\left(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b}\right).

Under the assumptions of Theorem
6
, we have
E\delta_{11}=2\tau+o(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Proof.

Let
R_{j}=\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi},I_{j}=I\left(p(\mathbf{z}_{j})>\tilde{b}\right),S_{2}(u)=E(f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}|\mathbf{z}_{i})\bigg|_{\mathbf{z}_{i}=u}=E(f_{i}(1,\pi)-f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\bigg|_{\mathbf{z}_{i}=u}
.

\displaystyle E\delta_{11}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{n\tilde{h}^{p}}E\left\{\frac{1}{p(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(0\right)f_{i}\left(W_{i},\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{i}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{i}}{1-\pi}\right)I\left(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{2n(n-1)}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{p}}E\left\{\frac{1}{p(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{2}\left(W_{2},\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{2}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{2}}{1-\pi}\right)I\left(p(\mathbf{z}_{1})>\tilde{b}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\right)+\left(2-\frac{2}{n}\right)E\left[\frac{I_{1}}{\tilde{h}^{p}p(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{2})\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\right)+\left(2-\frac{2}{n}\right)\iint{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})>\tilde{b})}K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{z}_{1}d\mathbf{z}_{2}.

By Taylor expansion, we have
p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})=p(\mathbf{z}_{2})+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}^{\top}\nabla p(\xi)
, where
\xi
is a vector in the neighbourhood of
\mathbf{z}_{2}
. By Assumption
5
,
\nabla p(\xi)
is bounded and
K(\mathbf{u})=0
for
u\in\{u:|u|_{\infty}\geq 1\}
, therefore,
|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\nabla p(\xi)|
is bounded. Moreover,
\tilde{h}=o(\tilde{b})
, therefore,

\displaystyle\iint_{p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u})<\tilde{b}}K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{2}\lesssim\iint_{p(\mathbf{z}_{2})<1.01\tilde{b}}K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)|S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{2})|p(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{p(\mathbf{z}_{2})<1.01\tilde{b}}|S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{2})|p(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{z}_{2}=o(n^{-1/2}),

(101)

where in the last equality we use Assumption
5
.
Note that
\int S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{z}_{2}=\tau
. By combining all the equations above, we have

\displaystyle E\delta_{11}

\displaystyle=O\left(\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\right)+\left(2-\frac{2}{n}\right)\iint K\left(\mathbf{u}\right)S_{2}(\mathbf{z}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{u}d\mathbf{z}_{2}+o(n^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=o(n^{-1/2})+2\tau.

∎

Lemma 15
.

Let

\displaystyle\delta_{21}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\frac{I(p(\mathbf{z}_{i})>\tilde{b})}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)\left(\frac{W_{j}W_{k}}{\pi^{2}}-\frac{(1-W_{j})(1-W_{k})}{(1-\pi)^{2}}\right).

Under the assumptions of Theorem
6
, we have
E\delta_{21}=\tau+o(n^{-\frac{1}{2}})
.

Proof.

Let
R_{j}=\frac{W_{j}}{\pi}-\frac{1-W_{j}}{1-\pi},I_{j}=I\left(p(\mathbf{z}_{j})>\tilde{b}\right),S_{1}(u)=E(f_{i}(W_{i},\pi)R_{i}|\mathbf{z}_{i})\bigg|_{\mathbf{z}_{i}=u}=E(f_{i}(1,\pi)-f_{i}(0,\pi)|\mathbf{z}_{i})\bigg|_{\mathbf{z}_{i}=u}
. We derive that

\displaystyle E\delta_{21}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sum_{k=1}^{n}E\left\{\frac{I_{i}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{i})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{j}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{i}-\mathbf{z}_{k}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{j}\left(W_{j},\pi\right)R_{j}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{n(n-1)(n-2)}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}E\left\{\frac{I_{1}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{3}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{2}\left(W_{2},\pi\right)R_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{n(n-1)}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}E\left\{\frac{I_{1}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K\left(0\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{3}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{1}\left(W_{1},\pi\right)R_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{n(n-1)}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}E\left\{\frac{I_{1}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(0\right)f_{2}\left(W_{2},\pi\right)R_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{n(n-1)}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}E\left\{\frac{I_{1}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K^{2}\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)f_{2}\left(W_{2},\pi\right)R_{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{n}{n^{3}\tilde{h}^{2p}}E\left\{\frac{I_{1}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K^{2}\left(0\right)f_{1}\left(W_{1},\pi\right)R_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=A+B+C+D+E.

For
B,C,D,E
, similar to the arguments in Lemma
13
, we have

B=O\left(\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\right),C=O\left(\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\right),D=O\left(\frac{1}{n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b}}\right),E=O\left(\frac{1}{n^{2}\tilde{h}^{2p}\tilde{b}^{2}}\right).

By Assumption
5
,
\sqrt{n}=o(n\tilde{h}^{p}\tilde{b})
, therefore,

B=o\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right),C=o\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right),D=o\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right),E=o\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

For
A
, we derive that

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\left(1-O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\frac{1}{\tilde{h}^{2p}}E\left\{\frac{I_{1}}{p^{2}(\mathbf{z}_{1})}K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{2}}{\tilde{h}}\right)K\left(\frac{\mathbf{z}_{1}-\mathbf{z}_{3}}{\tilde{h}}\right)S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{2})\right\}

\displaystyle=\left(1-O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\iiint\frac{p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})}{p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})}I(p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})K(\mathbf{u}_{1})K(\mathbf{u}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{z}_{2}d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}.

By (
Proof.
), we have

\displaystyle\iiint I(p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})K(\mathbf{u}_{1})K(\mathbf{u}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})|S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{2})|d\mathbf{z}_{2}d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}=o(1/\sqrt{n}).

(102)

By (
102
) and the fact that
\tilde{h}=o(\tilde{b})
, we have

\displaystyle\iiint\frac{\tilde{h}}{p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})}I(p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})K(\mathbf{u}_{1})K(\mathbf{u}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})|S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{2})|d\mathbf{z}_{2}d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}=o(1/\sqrt{n}).

(103)

Since
p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2})-p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})=\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{2}^{\top}\nabla p(\xi)
, and
|\mathbf{u}^{\top}\nabla p(\xi)|
is bounded, by (
103
), we have

\displaystyle\iiint\frac{|p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1}+h\mathbf{u}_{2})-p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})|}{p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})}I(p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})K(\mathbf{u}_{1})K(\mathbf{u}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})|S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{2})|d\mathbf{z}_{2}d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}=o(1/\sqrt{n}).

(104)

By (
104
) and the Jensen’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\left(1-O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\left[\iiint I(p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})K(\mathbf{u}_{1})K(\mathbf{u}_{2})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{z}_{2}d\mathbf{u}_{1}d\mathbf{u}_{2}+o(1/\sqrt{n})\right]

\displaystyle=\left(1-O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\left[\iint I(p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})>\tilde{b})K(\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{z}_{2}d\mathbf{u}_{1}+o(1/\sqrt{n})\right]

\displaystyle=\left(1-O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\left[\tau-\iint I(p(\mathbf{z}_{2}+\tilde{h}\mathbf{u}_{1})\leq\tilde{b})K(\mathbf{u}_{1})p(\mathbf{z}_{2})S_{1}(\mathbf{z}_{2})d\mathbf{z}_{2}d\mathbf{u}_{1}+o(1/\sqrt{n})\right]

\displaystyle=\tau+o(1/\sqrt{n}),

where in the last equality we use (
Proof.
). Then the result follows.
∎
